\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{funcD01a()}{funcD01a()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \+\_\+\+\_\+global\+\_\+\+\_\+ void func\+D01a (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{N,  }\item[{int}]{zdim,  }\item[{int}]{sdim,  }\item[{int}]{phidecdim,  }\item[{float}]{zdelta,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{massloading\+\_\+loc\+\_\+source\+\_\+phiD,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ttlmlD,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{source\+ZD,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{centX,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{centY,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{cloud\+\_\+sigma2,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{locX,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{locY,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{locZ,  }\item[{float \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{massreleased }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Compute partial mass loading for each (location, source, phi) element (= lsmpl). 

Each CUDA thread computes one flattened element of\+: \begin{DoxyVerb}massloading_loc_source_phiD[location, source, phidec]
\end{DoxyVerb}
 Flattened layout\+: \begin{DoxyVerb}tid = ((location * sdim) + source) * phidecdim + phidec
\end{DoxyVerb}
 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em N} & Total number of flattened elements\+: locdim \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} sdim \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} phidecdim \\
\hline
{\em zdim} & Number of vertical grid intervals \\
\hline
{\em sdim} & Number of source points \\
\hline
{\em phidecdim} & Number of grain-\/size subdivisions \\
\hline
{\em zdelta} & Vertical grid spacing \\
\hline
{\em massloading\+\_\+loc\+\_\+source\+\_\+phiD} & Output partial mass loading per location/source/phi \\
\hline
{\em ttlmlD} & Output total mass loading buffer, used by func\+D01b \\
\hline
{\em source\+ZD} & Source height array \\
\hline
{\em centX} & Deposit center X array indexed by (phidec, source, z) \\
\hline
{\em centY} & Deposit center Y array indexed by (phidec, source, z) \\
\hline
{\em cloud\+\_\+sigma2} & Variance array indexed by (phidec, source, z) \\
\hline
{\em locX} & Location X array for the current chunk \\
\hline
{\em locY} & Location Y array for the current chunk \\
\hline
{\em locZ} & Location Z array for the current chunk \\
\hline
{\em massreleased} & Released mass indexed by (phidec, source) \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{main_8cu_ad4ee0b5a45471bab63d687f3dd8422f5}{\+\_\+\+\_\+global\+\_\+\+\_\+}} \mbox{\hyperlink{main_8cu_ad4ee0b5a45471bab63d687f3dd8422f5}{void}} func\+D01b (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{main_8cu_ad4ee0b5a45471bab63d687f3dd8422f5}{int}}}]{N,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{main_8cu_ad4ee0b5a45471bab63d687f3dd8422f5}{int}}}]{locdim,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{main_8cu_ad4ee0b5a45471bab63d687f3dd8422f5}{float}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{massloading\+\_\+loc\+\_\+source\+\_\+phiD,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{main_8cu_ad4ee0b5a45471bab63d687f3dd8422f5}{float}} \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{ttlmlD }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Reduce partial mass loading over source and phi for each location. 

func\+D01a produces\+: \begin{DoxyVerb}massloading_loc_source_phiD[location, source, phidec]
\end{DoxyVerb}
 This kernel sums all source/phi contributions for each location\+: \begin{DoxyVerb}ttlmlD[location] = sum over source and phidec
\end{DoxyVerb}
 
\begin{DoxyParams}{Parameters}
{\em N} & Total number of flattened massloading\+\_\+loc\+\_\+source\+\_\+phiD elements\+: locdim \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} sdim \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} phidecdim \\
\hline
{\em locdim} & Number of locations in the current chunk \\
\hline
{\em massloading\+\_\+loc\+\_\+source\+\_\+phiD} & Partial mass loading array \\
\hline
{\em ttlmlD} & Output total mass loading per location \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}
